## Narration 1126

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1136)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ نِسَاءً، كُنَّ فِي عَهْدِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُسْلِمْنَ بِأَرْضِهِنَّ وَهُنَّ غَيْرُ مُهَاجِرَاتٍ وَأَزْوَاجُهُنَّ حِينَ أَسْلَمْنَ كُفَّارٌ مِنْهُنَّ بِنْتُ الْوَلِيدِ بْنِ الْمُغِيرَةِ ‏.‏ وَكَانَتْ تَحْتَ صَفْوَانَ بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ فَأَسْلَمَتْ يَوْمَ الْفَتْحِ وَهَرَبَ زَوْجُهَا صَفْوَانُ بْنُ أُمَيَّةَ مِنَ الإِسْلاَمِ فَبَعَثَ إِلَيْهِ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ابْنَ عَمِّهِ وَهْبَ بْنَ عُمَيْرٍ بِرِدَاءِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَمَانًا لِصَفْوَانَ بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ وَدَعَاهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِلَى الإِسْلاَمِ وَأَنْ يَقْدَمَ عَلَيْهِ فَإِنْ رَضِيَ أَمْرًا قَبِلَهُ وَإِلاَّ سَيَّرَهُ شَهْرَيْنِ فَلَمَّا قَدِمَ صَفْوَانُ عَلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِرِدَائِهِ نَادَاهُ عَلَى رُءُوسِ النَّاسِ فَقَالَ يَا مُحَمَّدُ إِنَّ هَذَا وَهْبَ بْنَ عُمَيْرٍ جَاءَنِي بِرِدَائِكَ وَزَعَمَ أَنَّكَ دَعَوْتَنِي إِلَى الْقُدُومِ عَلَيْكَ فَإِنْ رَضِيتُ أَمْرًا قَبِلْتُهُ وَإِلاَّ سَيَّرْتَنِي شَهْرَيْنِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ انْزِلْ أَبَا وَهْبٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لاَ وَاللَّهِ لاَ أَنْزِلُ حَتَّى تُبَيِّنَ لِي ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ بَلْ لَكَ تَسِيرُ أَرْبَعَةَ أَشْهُرٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَخَرَجَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قِبَلَ هَوَازِنَ بِحُنَيْنٍ ‏.‏ فَأَرْسَلَ إِلَى صَفْوَانَ بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ يَسْتَعِيرُهُ أَدَاةً وَسِلاَحًا عِنْدَهُ فَقَالَ صَفْوَانُ أَطَوْعًا أَمْ كَرْهًا فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ بَلْ طَوْعًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَأَعَارَهُ الأَدَاةَ وَالسِّلاَحَ الَّتِي عِنْدَهُ ثُمَّ خَرَجَ صَفْوَانُ مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَهُوَ كَافِرٌ فَشَهِدَ حُنَيْنًا وَالطَّائِفَ وَهُوَ كَافِرٌ وَامْرَأَتُهُ مُسْلِمَةٌ وَلَمْ يُفَرِّقْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بَيْنَهُ وَبَيْنَ امْرَأَتِهِ حَتَّى أَسْلَمَ صَفْوَانُ وَاسْتَقَرَّتْ عِنْدَهُ امْرَأَتُهُ بِذَلِكَ النِّكَاحِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1136]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1136" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab that he had heard that in the time of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, women were becoming muslim in their own lands and they did not do hijra while their husbands were still kafirun although they themselves had become muslim. Among them was the daughter of al-Walid ibn al-Mughira and she was the wife of Safwan ibn Umayya. She became muslim on the day of the conquest (of Makka), and her husband, Safwan ibn Umayya fled from Islam. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, sent Safwan&#x27;s paternal cousin, Wahb ibn Umayr with the cloak of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, as a safe-conduct for Safwan ibn Umayya, and the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, called him to Islam and asked for him to come to him and if he was pleased with the matter to accept it. If not he would have a respite for two months. When Safwan came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, with his cloak, he called out to him over the heads of the people, &quot;Muhammad! Wahb ibn Umayr brought me your cloak and claimed that you had summoned me to come to you and if I was pleased with the matter, I should accept it and if not, you would give me a respite for two months. &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Come down, Abu Wahb.&quot; He said, &quot;No, by Allah! I will not come down until you make it clear to me.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;You have a respite of four months.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, went out toward Hawazin at Hunayn. He sent to Safwan ibn Umayya to borrow some equipment and arms that he had. Safwan said, &quot;Willingly or unwillingly?&quot; He said, &quot;Willingly.&quot; Therefore he lent him the equipment and arms which he had. Then Safwan went out with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, while he was still a kafir. He was present at the battles of Hunayn and at-Ta&#x27;if while he was still a kafir and his wife was a muslim. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, did not separate Safwan and his wife until he had become muslim, and his wife was settled with him by that marriage</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1136]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1136" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इब्न-ए-शिहाब से रिवायत है कि चाँद औरतें रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के ज़माने में मुसलमान हो जाती थीं, अपने मुल्क में हिजरत नहीं करती थीं और खावंद उनके काफिर होते थे. इन्हीं औरतों में से एक आतिका थीं जो वलीद बिन मुग़ीराह की बेटी थीं, जो सफवान बिन उमय्यह के निकाह में थीं. वह फ़तह-ए-मक्का के रोज़ मुसलमान हुईं और उनके खावंद सफवान भाग गये. तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उनके चाचा-ज़द भाई वहब बिन उमैर को अपनी चादर निशानी के वास्ते दे कर सफवान के पास भेजा और उनको अमन दी और इस्लाम की तरफ बुलाया और यह कहा कि मेरे पास आये, अगर तुम्हारी ख़ुशी हो तो मुसलमान होना, ना तो तुमको दो महीने की मुहलत मिलेगी. जब सफवान रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आपकी चादर ले कर आये तो लोगों के सामने पुकार उठे &#x27;ऐ मुहम्मद, वहब बिन उमैर मेरे पास तुम्हारी चादर ले कर आये और मुझसे कहा कि तुमने मुझको बुलाया है इस शर्त पर कि अगर मैं चाहूँ तो मुसलमान हो जाऊँ, नहीं तो मुझको दो महीने की मुहलत मिलेगी.&#x27; आपने फ़रमाया उतरो ऐ अबू वहब. सफवान ने कहा क़सम अल्लाह की मैं कभी न उतरूँ गा जब तक तुम मुझसे बयान न करोगे कि वहब बिन उमैर का पयाम सही है. आपने फ़रमाया वह तो क्या, मैं तुम्हें चार महीने की मुहलत देता हूँ. फिर रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) क़बीला-ए-हवाज़िन की तरफ हुनैन में गये और आपने सफवान से कुछ हथियार और सामान आरीअतन माँगा, सफवान ने कहा आप ख़ुशी से माँगते हैं या ज़बरदस्ती से? आपने फ़रमाया ख़ुशी से. सफवान ने हथियार और सामान दिये. फिर रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) लौटे और सफवान कुफ़्र ही की हालत में आपके साथ रहे, मगर आपने उनकी औरत को उनसे न छुड़ाया यहाँ तक कि सफवान भी मुसलमान हो गये और उनकी औरत बदस्तूर उनके पास रहीं. इब्न-ए-शिहाब ने कहा कि सफवान की बीवी खावंद से एक महीने पहले इस्लाम लायीं थीं और जो औरत दार-उल-कुफ़्र से मुसलमान हो कर दार-उल-इस्लाम में हिजरत कर के आये तो वह अपने खावंद से जुदा हो जाये गी और इद्दत कर के दूसरा निकाह कर ले गी, मगर जब उसका खावंद उसकी इद्दत के अंदर मुसलमान हो कर दार-उल-इस्लाम में आ जाये.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1136]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابن شہاب سے روایت ہے کہ چند عورتیں رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے زمانے میں مسلمان ہو جاتی تھیں اپنے ملک میں ہجرت نہیں کرتی تھیں اور خاوند ان کے کافر ہوتے تھے انہی عورتوں میں سے ایک عاتکہ تھی جو ولید بن مغیرہ کی بیٹی تھیں جو صفوان بن امیہ کے نکاح میں تھیں وہ فتح مکہ کے روز مسلمان ہوئیں اور ان کے خاوند صفوان بھاگ گئے تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ان کے چچا زاد بھائی وہب بن عمیر کو اپنی چادر نشانی کے واسطے دے کو صفوان کے پاس بھیجا اور ان کو امان دی اور اسلام کی طرف بلایا اور یہ کہا کہ میرے پاس آئے اگر تمہای خوشی ہو تو مسلمان ہونا نہ تو تم کو دو مہینے کی مہلت ملے گی جب صفوان رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس آپ کی چادر لے کر آ آئے تو لوگوں کے سامنے پکار اٹھے اے محمد وہب بن عمیر میرے پاس تمہاری چادر لے کر آئے اور مجھ سے کہا کہ تم نے مجھ کو بلایا ہے اس شرط پر کہ اگر میں چاہوں تو مسلمان ہو جاؤں نہیں تو مجھ کو دو مہینے کی مہلت ملے گی ۔ آپ نے فرمایا اترو اے ابووہب صفوان نے کہا قسم اللہ کی میں کبھی نہ اتروں گا جب تک تم مجھ سے بیان نہ کرو گے کہ وہب بن عمیر کا پیام صحیح ہے آپ نے فرمایا وہ تو کیا میں تمہیں چار مہینے کی مہلت دیتا ہوں پھر رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم قبیلہ ہوازن کی طرف حنین میں گئے اور آپ نے صفوان سے کچھ ہتھیار اور سامان عاریتاً مانگا صفوان نے کہا آپ خوشی سے مانگتے ہیں یا زبردستی سے آپ نے فرمایا خوشی سے صفوان نے ہتھیار اور سامان دئیے پھر رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم لوٹے اور صفوان کفر ہی کی حالت میں آپ کے ساتھ رہے مگر آپ نے ان کی عورت کو ان سے نہ چھڑایا یہاں تک کہ صفوان بھی مسلمان ہوگئے اور ان کی عورت بدستور ان کے پاس رہیں۔ ابن شہاب نے کہا کہ صفوان کی بی بی خاوند سے ایک مہینہ پہلے اسلام لائی تھیں اور جو عورت دارالکفر سے مسلمان ہو کر دارالا سلام میں ہجرت کر کے آئے تو وہ اپنے خاوند سے جدا ہو جائے گی اور عدت کر کے دوسرا نکاح کر لے گی مگر جب اس کا خاوند اس کی عدت کے اندر مسلمان ہو کر دارالاسلام میں آ جائے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1136]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn-e-Shihab se riwayat hai ki chand auratein Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke zamane mein musalman ho jati thin, apne mulk mein hijrat nahi karti thin aur khawand unke kafir hote the. Inhi auraton mein se ek Aatika thin jo Waleed bin Mughirah ki beti thin, jo Safwan bin Umayyah ke nikah mein thin. Woh Fateh-e-Makkah ke roz musalman huin aur unke khawand Safwan bhag gaye. Toh Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne unke chacha-zad bhai Wahb bin Umair ko apni chadar nishani ke waste de kar Safwan ke paas bheja aur unko aman di aur Islam ki taraf bulaya aur yeh kaha ki mere paas aaye, agar tumhari khushi ho toh musalman hona, na toh tumko do mahine ki muhlat milegi. Jab Safwan Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aapki chadar le kar aaye toh logon ke samne pukar uthe &#x27;Ae Muhammad, Wahb bin Umair mere paas tumhari chadar le kar aaye aur mujhse kaha ki tumne mujhko bulaya hai is shart par ki agar main chahu toh musalman ho jaun, nahi toh mujhko do mahine ki muhlat milegi.&#x27; Aapne farmaya utro ae Abu Wahb. Safwan ne kaha qasam Allah ki main kabhi na utru ga jab tak tum mujhse bayan na karoge ki Wahb bin Umair ka payam sahi hai. Aapne farmaya woh toh kya, main tumhe char mahine ki muhlat deta hun. Phir Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) qabeela-e-Hawazin ki taraf Hunain mein gaye aur aapne Safwan se kuch hathyar aur saman aariatan manga, Safwan ne kaha aap khushi se mangte hain ya zabardasti se? Aapne farmaya khushi se. Safwan ne hathyar aur saman diye. Phir Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) laute aur Safwan kufr hi ki halat mein aapke sath rahe, magar aapne unki aurat ko unse na chhudaya yahan tak ki Safwan bhi musalman ho gaye aur unki aurat badastoor unke paas rahin. Ibn-e-Shihab ne kaha ki Safwan ki biwi khawand se ek mahine pehle Islam layin thin aur jo aurat Dar-ul-Kufr se musalman ho kar Dar-ul-Islam mein hijrat kar ke aaye toh woh apne khawand se juda ho jaye gi aur iddat kar ke dusra nikah kar le gi, magar jab uska khawand uski iddat ke andar musalman ho kar Dar-ul-Islam mein aa jaye.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1136]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1136" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ كَانَ بَيْنَ إِسْلاَمِ صَفْوَانَ وَبَيْنَ إِسْلاَمِ امْرَأَتِهِ نَحْوٌ مِنْ شَهْرٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ وَلَمْ يَبْلُغْنَا أَنَّ امْرَأَةً هَاجَرَتْ إِلَى اللَّهِ وَرَسُولِهِ وَزَوْجُهَا كَافِرٌ مُقِيمٌ بِدَارِ الْكُفْرِ إِلاَّ فَرَّقَتْ هِجْرَتُهَا بَيْنَهَا وَبَيْنَ زَوْجِهَا إِلاَّ أَنْ يَقْدَمَ زَوْجُهَا مُهَاجِرًا قَبْلَ أَنْ تَنْقَضِيَ عِدَّتُهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1137" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Safwan (unresolved). The first source statement reports a concrete conversion interval involving Safwan and his wife. The subsequent general exposition about legal effects of migration remains context; no canonical identity is reconstructed.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Ibn Shihab said, &quot;Between the Islam of Safwan and the Islam of his wife there was about one month.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1137]</p>

<p dir="ltr"> Ibn Shihab said, &quot;We have not heard about any woman doing hijra for Allah and His Messenger while her husband was a kafir abiding in the land of kufr, but that her hijra separated her and her husband unless her husband came in hijra before her period of idda had been completed</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत की, इब्न शिहाब ने कहा: सफवान के इस्लाम और उनकी बीवी के इस्लाम के दरम्यान तक़रीबन एक माह का वक़फ़ा था. इब्न शिहाब ने कहा: हम ने किसी ऐसी औरत के बारे में नहीं सुना जो अल्लाह और उसके रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की ख़ातिर हिजरत करे जबके उसका शौहर काफिर हो और दार-उल-कुफ़्र में रहता हो, के उसकी हिजरत ने उसे और उसके शौहर को अलग न किया हो, सिवाये उसके के उसका शौहर उसकी इद्दत मुकम्मल होने से पहले हिजरत कर के आ जाये.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے روایت کی، ابن شہاب نے کہا: صفوان کے اسلام اور ان کی بیوی کے اسلام کے درمیان تقریباً ایک ماہ کا وقفہ تھا۔ ابن شہاب نے کہا: ہم نے کسی ایسی عورت کے بارے میں نہیں سنا جو اللہ اور اس کے رسول (صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم) کی خاطر ہجرت کرے جبکہ اس کا شوہر کافر ہو اور دارالکفر میں رہتا ہو، کہ اس کی ہجرت نے اسے اور اس کے شوہر کو الگ نہ کیا ہو، سوائے اس کے کہ اس کا شوہر اس کی عدت مکمل ہونے سے پہلے ہجرت کر کے آ جائے۔</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se riwayat ki, Ibn Shihab ne kaha: Safwan ke Islam aur unki biwi ke Islam ke darmiyan taqreeban ek mah ka waqfa tha. Ibn Shihab ne kaha: Hum ne kisi aisi aurat ke bare mein nahi suna jo Allah aur Uske Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki khatir hijrat kare jabke uska shohar kafir ho aur dar-ul-kufr mein rehta ho, ke uski hijrat ne use aur uske shohar ko alag na kiya ho, siwaye uske ke uska shohar uski iddat mukammal hone se pehle hijrat kar ke aa jaye.</p>

## Narration 1128

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1138]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1138" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1138</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1138 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّ أُمَّ حَكِيمٍ بِنْتَ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ هِشَامٍ، وَكَانَتْ، تَحْتَ عِكْرِمَةَ بْنِ أَبِي جَهْلٍ فَأَسْلَمَتْ يَوْمَ الْفَتْحِ وَهَرَبَ زَوْجُهَا عِكْرِمَةُ بْنُ أَبِي جَهْلٍ مِنَ الإِسْلاَمِ حَتَّى قَدِمَ الْيَمَنَ فَارْتَحَلَتْ أُمُّ حَكِيمٍ حَتَّى قَدِمَتْ عَلَيْهِ بِالْيَمَنِ فَدَعَتْهُ إِلَى الإِسْلاَمِ فَأَسْلَمَ وَقَدِمَ عَلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَامَ الْفَتْحِ فَلَمَّا رَآهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَثَبَ إِلَيْهِ فَرِحًا وَمَا عَلَيْهِ رِدَاءٌ حَتَّى بَايَعَهُ فَثَبَتَا عَلَى نِكَاحِهِمَا ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِذَا أَسْلَمَ الرَّجُلُ قَبْلَ امْرَأَتِهِ وَقَعَتِ الْفُرْقَةُ بَيْنَهُمَا إِذَا عُرِضَ عَلَيْهَا الإِسْلاَمُ فَلَمْ تُسْلِمْ لأَنَّ اللَّهَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى يَقُولُ فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏وَلاَ تُمْسِكُوا بِعِصَمِ الْكَوَافِرِ‏}‏‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1138" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab reporting Umm Hakim, Ikrima and the Messengers reception. Select the actual conversion, Yemen pursuit, return, welcoming and confirmation of marriage. The subsequent general ruling by Malik remains compiler commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that Umm Hakim bint al-Harith ibn Hisham who was the wife of Ikrima ibn Abi Jahl became muslim on the day of the conquest of Makka, and her husband Ikrima fled from Islam as far as the Yemen. Umm Hakim set out after him until she came to him in the Yemen and she called him to Islam, and he became muslim. He went to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, in the year of the conquest. When the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, saw him, he rushed to him in joy and did not bother to put on his cloak until he had made the pledge with him. They were confirmed in their marriage. </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1138]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1138" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1138</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1138 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;If a man becomes muslim before his wife, a separation occurs between them when he presents Islam to her and she does not become muslim, because Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, said in His Book, &#x27;Do not hold fast to the ties of women who are kafirun</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1138" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1138</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1138 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इब्न-ए-शिहाब से रिवायत है कि उम्म-ए-हकीम इकरमा बिन अबू जहल की बीवी फतह-ए-मक्का के रोज़ मुसलमान हुईं और उनके खावंद इकरमा यमन भाग गए। उम्म-ए-हकीम भी वहां चली गईं और उनको दीन-ए-इस्लाम की तरफ बुलाया, वह मुसलमान हो गए और उसी साल आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आयीं। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने जब उनको देखा तो खुशी से उठ खड़े हुए और उनसे बैअत ली, उस वक़्त आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के जिस्म-ए-शरीफ़ पर चादर न थी, फिर दोनों मियां-बीवी अपने निकाह पर क़ायम रहे। कहा मालिक ने जब मर्द अपनी बीवी से पहले मुसलमान हो जाए और बीवी से मुसलमान होने को कहा जाए और वह मुसलमान न हो तो निकाह फ़स्ख हो जाए गा, क्योंकि अल्लाह जल्ला जलालुहू अपनी किताब में फरमाता है मत अलाक़ा रखो काफिर औरतों से।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1138" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابن شہاب سے روایت ہے کہ ام حکیم عکرمہ بن ابوجہل کی بی بی فتح مکہ کے روز مسلمان ہوئی اور ان کے خاوند عکرمہ یمن بھاگ گئے ام حکیم بھی وہاں چلی گئیں اور ان کو دین اسلام کی طرف بلایا وہ مسلمان ہوگئے اور اسی سال آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس آئی آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے جب ان کو دیکھا تو خوشی سے اٹھ کھڑے ہوئے اور ان سے بیعت لی اس وقت آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے جسم شریف پر چادر نہ تھی پھر دونوں میاں بی بی اپنے نکاح پر قائم رہے ۔ کہا مالک نے جب مرد اپنی بی بی سے پہلے مسلمان ہو جائے اور بی بی سے مسلمان ہونے کو کہا جائے اور وہ مسلمان نہ ہو تو نکاح فسخ ہو جائے گا کیونکہ اللہ جل جلالہ اپنی کتاب میں فرماتا ہے مت علاقہ رکھو کافر عورتوں سے</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1138" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn-e-Shihab se riwayat hai ki Umm-e-Hakim Ikramah bin Abu Jahl ki biwi Fateh-e-Makkah ke roz musalman huin aur unke khawand Ikramah Yemen bhag gaye. Umm-e-Hakim bhi wahan chali gayin aur unko Deen-e-Islam ki taraf bulaya, woh musalman ho gaye aur usi saal aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aayi. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne jab unko dekha toh khushi se uth khade hue aur unse bai&#x27;at li, us waqt aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke jism-e-shareef par chadar na thi, phir dono miyan-biwi apne nikah par qaim rahe. Kaha Malik ne jab mard apni biwi se pehle musalman ho jaye aur biwi se musalman hone ko kaha jaye aur woh musalman na ho toh nikah faskh ho jaye ga, kyunki Allah Jalla Jalaluhu apni kitab mein farmata hai mat alaaqa rakho kafir auraton se.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1138" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1129

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1139)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ حُمَيْدٍ الطَّوِيلِ، عَنْ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنَ عَوْفٍ، جَاءَ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَبِهِ أَثَرُ صُفْرَةٍ فَسَأَلَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَأَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّهُ تَزَوَّجَ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ كَمْ سُقْتَ إِلَيْهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ زِنَةَ نَوَاةٍ مِنْ ذَهَبٍ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَوْلِمْ وَلَوْ بِشَاةٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1139]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1139" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1139</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1139 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Humayd at-Tawil from Anas ibn Malik that Abd ar-Rahman ibn Awf came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and he had a traceof yellow on him. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, asked about it. He told him that he had just been married. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;How much did you hand over to her?&quot; He said, &quot;The weight of a date pit in gold.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to him, &quot;Hold a feast, even if it is only with a sheep</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1139]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1139" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अनस बिन मालिक से रिवायत है कि अब्दुर रहमान बिन ऑफ रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आये और उनपर ज़र्दी का निशान था. रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पूछा यह क्या है? उन्होंने बयां किया कि मैंने एक अंसारी औरत से निकाह किया है. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया क्या मेहर दिया है? उन्होंने कहा एक गुठली बराबर सोना. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया वलीमा कर अगरचे एक बकरी का हो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1139]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1139" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">انس بن مالک سے روایت ہے کہ عبدالرحمن بن عوف رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس آئے اور ان پر زردی کا نشان تھا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے پوچھا یہ کیا ہے انہوں نے بیان کیا کہ میں نے ایک انصاری عورت سے نکاح کیا ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کیا مہر دیا ہے انہوں نے کہا ایک گٹھلی برابر سونا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا ولیمہ کر اگرچہ ایک بکری کا ہو۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1139]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1139" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Anas bin Malik se riwayat hai ki Abdur Rahman bin Auf Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aaye aur unpar zardi ka nishan tha. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne pucha yeh kya hai? Unhone bayan kiya ki maine ek Ansari aurat se nikah kiya hai. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya kya mehar diya hai? Unhone kaha ek guthli barabar sona. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya walima kar agarche ek bakri ka ho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1139]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1139" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1130

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ لَقَدْ بَلَغَنِي أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ يُولِمُ بِالْوَلِيمَةِ مَا فِيهَا خُبْزٌ وَلاَ لَحْمٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1140]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1140" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

## Narration 1131

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1141)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا دُعِيَ أَحَدُكُمْ إِلَى وَلِيمَةٍ فَلْيَأْتِهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1141]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1141" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1141</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1141 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;When you are invited to a wedding feast, you must go to it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1141]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1141" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया में से कोई वलीमा की दावत में बुलाया जाये तोह हाज़िर हो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1141]</p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ شَرُّ الطَّعَامِ طَعَامُ الْوَلِيمَةِ يُدْعَى لَهَا الأَغْنِيَاءُ وَيُتْرَكُ الْمَسَاكِينُ وَمَنْ لَمْ يَأْتِ الدَّعْوَةَ فَقَدْ عَصَى اللَّهَ وَرَسُولَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1142" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Hurayra (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibr. Shihab from al-Araj that Abu Hurayra said, &quot;The worst food is the food of a wedding feast to which the rich are invited and the poor are left out. If anyone rejects an invitation, he has rebelled against Allah and His Messenger</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1142]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1142" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते थे वलीमा का खाना सब खाने से बुरा है फ़क़ीर छोड़ दिए जाते हैं और जो शख्स दावत में न आये उसने नाफ़रमानी की अल्लाह और रसूल की.</p>

## Narration 1133

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1143)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ إِسْحَاقَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي طَلْحَةَ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ أَنَسَ بْنَ مَالِكٍ، يَقُولُ إِنَّ خَيَّاطًا دَعَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لِطَعَامٍ صَنَعَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَنَسٌ فَذَهَبْتُ مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِلَى ذَلِكَ الطَّعَامِ فَقَرَّبَ إِلَيْهِ خُبْزًا مِنْ شَعِيرٍ وَمَرَقًا فِيهِ دُبَّاءُ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَنَسٌ فَرَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَتَتَبَّعُ الدُّبَّاءَ مِنْ حَوْلِ الْقَصْعَةِ فَلَمْ أَزَلْ أُحِبُّ الدُّبَّاءَ بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ الْيَوْمِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1143]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1143" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1143</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1143 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Ishaq ibn Abdullah ibn Abi Talha heard Anas ibn Malik say that a certain tailor invited the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, to eat some food which he had prepared. Anas said, &quot;I went with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, to eat the food. He served barley bread and a soup with pumpkin in it. I saw the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, going after the pumpkin around the dish, so I have always liked pumpkin since that day</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1143]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1143" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1143 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अनस बिन मालिक कहते थे के एक दर्जी ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की कुछ खाना पका कर दावत की भी रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ गया. वह दर्ज़ी जौ की रोटी और कद्दू का सालन सामने लाया तोह मैं ने देखा के आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) प्याले में से कद्दू धुंध कर खाते थे को पसंद करने लगा.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1143]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1143" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">انس بن مالک کہتے تھے کہ ایک درزی نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی کچھ کھانا پکا کر دعوت کی میں بھی رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ گیا وہ درزی جو کی روٹی اور کدو کا سالن سامنے لایا تو میں نے دیکھا کہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم پیالے میں سے کدو ڈھونڈھ کر کھاتے تھے اس روز سے میں بھی کدؤں کو پسند کرنے لگا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1143]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1143" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Anas bin Malik kehte the ke ek darzi ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki kuch khana paka kar dawat ki, main bhi Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke sath gaya. Woh darzi jau ki roti aur kaddu ka saalan samne laya toh main ne dekha ke aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) pyale mein se kaddu dhundh kar khate the, us roz se main bhi kadduon ko pasand karne laga.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1143]</p>

## Narration 1134

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1144)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا تَزَوَّجَ أَحَدُكُمُ الْمَرْأَةَ أَوِ اشْتَرَى الْجَارِيَةَ فَلْيَأْخُذْ بِنَاصِيَتِهَا وَلْيَدْعُ بِالْبَرَكَةِ وَإِذَا اشْتَرَى الْبَعِيرَ فَلْيَأْخُذْ بِذِرْوَةِ سَنَامِهِ وَلْيَسْتَعِذْ بِاللَّهِ مِنَ الشَّيْطَانِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1144]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1144" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1144</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1144 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;When you marry a woman or buy a slave-girl, take her by the forelock and ask for baraka. When you buy a camel, take the top of its hump, and seek refuge with Allah from Shaytan</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1144]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1144" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1144</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1144 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन असलाम से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया निकाह करे या लोंडी खरीदे तोह उसकी पेशानी पकड़ कर बरकत की दुआ करे और जब ऊँट खरीदे तोह उसकी कोहन पर हाथ रखे और शैतान मरदूद से पनाह मांगे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1144]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1144" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن اسلم سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جب تم میں سے کوئی کسی عورت سے نکاح کرے یا لونڈی خریدے تو اس کی پیشانی پکڑ کر برکت کی دعا کرے اور جب اونٹ خریدے تو اس کی کوہان پر ہاتھ رکھے اور شیطان مردود سے پناہ مانگے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1144]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1144" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Aslam se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, jab tum mein se koi kisi aurat se nikah kare ya londi khareede toh uski peshani pakad kar barkat ki dua kare aur jab oont khareede toh uski kohan par hath rakhe aur shaitan mardood se panah mange.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1144]</p>

## Narration 1135

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1145)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ الْمَكِّيِّ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، خَطَبَ إِلَى رَجُلٍ أُخْتَهُ فَذَكَرَ أَنَّهَا قَدْ كَانَتْ أَحْدَثَتْ فَبَلَغَ ذَلِكَ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ فَضَرَبَهُ - أَوْ كَادَ يَضْرِبُهُ - ثُمَّ قَالَ مَا لَكَ وَلِلْخَبَرِ</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z-Zubayr al-Makki that somebody asked a man for his sister in marriage and the man mentioned that she had committed fornication. Umar ibn al-Khattab heard about it and he beat the man or almost beat him, and said, &quot;What did you mean by giving him such information?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1145]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1145" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबू जुबैर मक्की से रिवायत है के एक शख्श ने निकाह का पयाम दिया एक शख्श की बहन को, उसने बयान किया के वह औरत बदकार है। जब हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) को इसकी खबर पहुंची, आपने उस शख्श को बुला कर मारा या मारने का क़सद किया और कहा के तुझे इस खबर पहुँचाने से क्या ग़र्ज़ थी।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1145" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Zubair Makki se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs ne nikah ka payam diya ek shakhs ki behen ko, usne bayan kiya ke woh aurat badkar hai. Jab Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko iski khabar pahunchi, aapne us shakhs ko bula kar mara ya maarne ka qasd kiya aur kaha ke tujhe is khabar pahunchane se kya garz thi.</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ الْقَاسِمَ بْنَ مُحَمَّدٍ، وَعُرْوَةَ بْنَ الزُّبَيْرِ، كَانَا يَقُولاَنِ فِي الرَّجُلِ يَكُونُ عِنْدَهُ أَرْبَعُ نِسْوَةٍ فَيُطَلِّقُ إِحْدَاهُنَّ الْبَتَّةَ أَنَّهُ يَتَزَوَّجُ إِنْ شَاءَ وَلاَ يَنْتَظِرُ أَنْ تَنْقَضِيَ عِدَّتُهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1146" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: al-Qasim ibn Muhammad (successor); Urwa ibn az-Zubayr (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman that al-Qasim ibn Muhammad and Urwa ibn az-Zubayr said that a man who had four wives and then divorced one of them irrevocably, could marry straightaway if he wished, and he did not have to wait for the completion of her idda</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1146]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1146" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">राबिआ बिन अबू अब्दुर्रहमान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के क़सीम बिन मुहम्मद और उरवह बिन ज़ुबैर कहते थे जिस शख्स की चार औरतें हों फिर वह उसमें से एक औरत को तीन तलाक़ दे दे तोह एक औरत नयी कर सकता है गुजरने का इंतज़ार ज़रूरी नहीं. क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद ने यह कहा कि उस औरत को कई मजलिसों में तलाक़ दी हो.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1146" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ربیعہ بن ابو عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ قاسم بن محمد اور عروہ بن زبیر کہتے تھے جس شخص کی چار عورتیں ہوں پھر وہ اس میں سے ایک عورت کو تین طلاق دے دے تو ایک عورت نئی کر سکتا ہے اس کی عدت گزرنے کا انتظار ضروری نہیں ۔ ربیعہ بن ابو عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ قاسم بن محمد اور عروہ بن زبیر نے ولید بن عبدالملک کو جس سال وہ مدینہ میں آیا تھا ایسا ہی فتوی دیا تھا مگر قاسم بن محمد نے یہ کہا کہ اس عورت کو کئی مجلسوں میں طلاق دی ہو ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Rabia bin Abu Abdurrahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Qasim bin Muhammad aur Urwah bin Zubair kehte the jis shakhs ki chaar aurtein hon phir woh usmein se ek aurat ko teen talaq de de toh ek aurat nayi kar sakta hai, uski iddat guzarne ka intezar zaroori nahi. Qasim bin Muhammad ne yeh kaha ke us aurat ko kai majlison mein talaq di ho.</p>

## Narration 1137

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1147)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1147]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1147" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1147</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1147 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ الْقَاسِمَ بْنَ مُحَمَّدٍ، وَعُرْوَةَ بْنَ الزُّبَيْرِ، أَفْتَيَا الْوَلِيدَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ عَامَ قَدِمَ الْمَدِينَةَ بِذَلِكَ غَيْرَ أَنَّ الْقَاسِمَ بْنَ مُحَمَّدٍ قَالَ طَلَّقَهَا فِي مَجَالِسَ شَتَّى ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1147" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: al-Qasim ibn Muhammad (successor); Urwa ibn az-Zubayr (successor). The source explicitly refers to a previous judgment and supplies a variant; the missing judgment is not imported into this exact quotation. The final gloss remains context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Rabia ibn Abd ar-Rahman that al-Qasim ibn Muhammad and Urwa ibn az-Zubayr gave the same judgement to al-Walid ibn Abd al-Malik in the year of his arrival in Madina except that al-Qasim ibn Muhammad said that he divorced his wife on various occasions.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1147]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1147" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से, उन्होंने रबीआ बिन अब्दुर-रहमान से रिवायत की के कासिम बिन मुहम्मद और उर्वा बिन अज़-ज़ुबैर ने वलीद बिन अब्दुल-मलिक को उसके मदीना आने के साल में एक जैसा फ़ैसला सुनाया, सिवाये उसके के कासिम बिन मुहम्मद ने कहा के उसने अपनी बीवी को मुख्तलिफ मवाक़े पर तलाक़ दी थी। (या&#x27;नी एक वक़्त में नहीं)</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1147" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Said ibn al-Musayyab said, &quot;There are three things in which there is no jest:marriage, divorce, and setting free</p>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब ने कहा के तीन चीज़ें ऐसी हैं जिन में खेल नहीं होता: निकाह, तलाक़ और एताक़ (आज़ाद करना)।</p>

## Narration 1139

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1149)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1149]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1149" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1149</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1149 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ رَافِعِ بْنِ خَدِيجٍ، أَنَّهُ تَزَوَّجَ بِنْتَ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ مَسْلَمَةَ الأَنْصَارِيِّ فَكَانَتْ عِنْدَهُ حَتَّى كَبِرَتْ فَتَزَوَّجَ عَلَيْهَا فَتَاةً شَابَّةً فَآثَرَ الشَّابَّةَ عَلَيْهَا فَنَاشَدَتْهُ الطَّلاَقَ فَطَلَّقَهَا وَاحِدَةً ثُمَّ أَمْهَلَهَا حَتَّى إِذَا كَادَتْ تَحِلُّ رَاجَعَهَا ثُمَّ عَادَ فَآثَرَ الشَّابَّةَ فَنَاشَدَتْهُ الطَّلاَقَ فَطَلَّقَهَا وَاحِدَةً ثُمَّ رَاجَعَهَا ثُمَّ عَادَ فَآثَرَ الشَّابَّةَ فَنَاشَدَتْهُ الطَّلاَقَ فَقَالَ مَا شِئْتِ إِنَّمَا بَقِيَتْ وَاحِدَةٌ فَإِنْ شِئْتِ اسْتَقْرَرْتِ عَلَى مَا تَرَيْنَ مِنَ الأُثْرَةِ وَإِنْ شِئْتِ فَارَقْتُكِ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ بَلْ أَسْتَقِرُّ عَلَى الأُثْرَةِ ‏.‏ فَأَمْسَكَهَا عَلَى ذَلِكَ وَلَمْ يَرَ رَافِعٌ عَلَيْهِ إِثْمًا حِينَ قَرَّتْ عِنْدَهُ عَلَى الأُثْرَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1149" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Rafi ibn Khadij (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that Rafi ibn Khadij married the daughter of Muhammad ibn Maslama al-Ansari. She was with him until she grew older, and then he married a young girl and preferred the young girl to her. She begged him to divorce her, so he divorced her and then he gave her time until she had almost finished her idda period and then he returned and still preferred the young girl. She therefore asked him to divorce her. He divorced her once, and then returned to her, and still preferred the young girl, and she asked him to divorce her. He said, &quot;What do you want? There is only one divorce left. If you like, continue and put up with what you see of preference, and if you like, I will separate from you.&quot; She said, &quot;I will continue in spite of the preference.&quot; He kept her in spite of that. Rafi did not see that he had done any wrong action when she remained with him in spite of preference</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1149]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1149" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1149]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1149" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1149</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1149 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">राफे बिन खदीज ने मुहम्मद बिन मसलमा अंसारी की बेटी से निकाह किया, वह उनके पास रहीं। जब बुढ़ियाँ हुईं तो राफे ने एक जवान औरत से निकाह किया तो उसकी तरफ़ ज़्यादा माइल हुए। तो बुढ़िया औरत ने तलाक़ मांगी, मुहम्मद बिन मसलमा ने एक तलाक़ दे दी, फिर जब इद्दत उसकी गुज़रने लगी रज़अत कर ली और जवान औरत की तरफ़ माइल रहे, फिर जब इद्दत गुज़रने लगी रज़अत कर ली और जवान औरत की तरफ़ माइल रहे। बुढ़िया ने फिर तलाक़ मांगी तब राफे बिन खदीज ने कहा अब तुझे क्या मंज़ूर है, एक तलाक़ और रह गयी है, अगर तू इस हाल में मेरे पास रहना चाहती है तो रह और अगर नहीं रह सकती तो मैं तुझे छोड़ दूँ। उसने कहा मुझे इसी हाल में रहना मंज़ूर है, राफे ने उसको रख लिया और अपने ऊपर कुछ गुनाह नहीं समझा।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1149" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">رافع بن خدیج نے محمد بن مسلمہ انصاری کی بیٹی سے نکاح کیا وہ ان کے پاس رہیں جب بڑھیاں ہوئیں تو رافع نے ایک جوان عورت سے نکاح کیا تو اس کی طرف زیادہ مائل ہوئے تو بڑھیا عورت نے طلاق مانگی محمد بن مسلمہ نے ایک طلاق دے دی پھر جب عدت اس کی گرزنے لگی رجعت کرلی اور جوان عورت کی طرف مائل رہے پھر جب عدت گرنے لگی رجعت کرلی اور جوان عورت کی طرف مائل رہے بڑھیا نے پھر طلاق مانگی تب رافع بن خدیج نے کہا اب تجھے کیا منظور ہے ایک طلاق اور رہ گئی ہے اگر تو اس حال میں میرے پاس رہنا چاہتی ہے تو رہ اور اگر نہیں رہ سکتی تو میں تجھے چھوڑ دوں اس نے کہا مجھے اسی حال میں رہنا منظور ہے رافع نے اس کو رکھ لیا اور اپنے اوپر کچھ گناہ نہیں سمجھا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1149" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1149 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Rafi bin Khadij ne Muhammad bin Maslamah Ansari ki beti se nikah kiya, woh unke paas rahin. Jab budhiyan huin toh Rafi ne ek jawan aurat se nikah kiya toh uski taraf zyada mail huye. Toh budhiya aurat ne talaq mangi, Muhammad bin Maslamah ne ek talaq de di, phir jab iddat uski guzarne lagi raj&#x27;at karli aur jawan aurat ki taraf mail rahe, phir jab iddat guzarne lagi raj&#x27;at karli aur jawan aurat ki taraf mail rahe. Budhiya ne phir talaq mangi tab Rafi bin Khadij ne kaha ab tujhe kya manzoor hai, ek talaq aur reh gayi hai, agar tu is haal mein mere paas rehna chahti hai toh reh aur agar nahi reh sakti toh main tujhe chhod doon. Usne kaha mujhe isi haal mein rehna manzoor hai, Rafi ne usko rakh liya aur apne upar kuch gunah nahi samjha.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1149" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1140

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَجُلاً، قَالَ لِعَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ إِنِّي طَلَّقْتُ امْرَأَتِي مِائَةَ تَطْلِيقَةٍ فَمَاذَا تَرَى عَلَىَّ فَقَالَ لَهُ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ طَلُقَتْ مِنْكَ لِثَلاَثٍ وَسَبْعٌ وَتِسْعُونَ اتَّخَذْتَ بِهَا آيَاتِ اللَّهِ هُزُوًا ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Abbas (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that a man said to Abdullah ibn Abbas, &quot;I have divorced my wife by saying I divorce you a hundred times. What do you think my situation is?&quot; Ibn Abbas said to him, &quot;She was divorced from you by three pronouncements, and by the ninety-seven, you have mocked the ayat of Allah</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1150]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1150" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत की कि उन्होंने सुना के एक आदमी ने अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास से कहा: मैंने अपनी बीवी को यह कह कर तलाक दी कि मैं तुझे सौ बार तलाक़ देता हूँ. आप इस बारे में क्या राय रखते हैं? इब्न अब्बास ने उस से कहा: वह तुम से तीन तलाक़ों से जुदा हो गयी</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se riwayat ki ke unhone suna ke ek aadmi ne Abdullah bin Abbas se kaha: Maine apni biwi ko yeh keh kar talaq di ke main tujhe sau baar talaq deta hoon. Aap is bare mein kya raye rakhte hain? Ibn Abbas ne us se kaha: Woh tum se teen talaqon se juda ho gayi, aur baqi satwanve se tum ne Allah ki ayaat ka mazaq udaya.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1150" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1141

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1151)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1151]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1151" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1151</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1151 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَجُلاً، جَاءَ إِلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ فَقَالَ إِنِّي طَلَّقْتُ امْرَأَتِي ثَمَانِيَ تَطْلِيقَاتٍ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ ابْنُ مَسْعُودٍ فَمَاذَا قِيلَ لَكَ قَالَ قِيلَ لِي إِنَّهَا قَدْ بَانَتْ مِنِّي ‏.‏ فَقَالَ ابْنُ مَسْعُودٍ صَدَقُوا مَنْ طَلَّقَ كَمَا أَمَرَهُ اللَّهُ فَقَدْ بَيَّنَ اللَّهُ لَهُ وَمَنْ لَبَسَ عَلَى نَفْسِهِ لَبْسًا جَعَلْنَا لَبْسَهُ مُلْصَقًا بِهِ لاَ تَلْبِسُوا عَلَى أَنْفُسِكُمْ وَنَتَحَمَّلَهُ عَنْكُمْ هُوَ كَمَا يَقُولُونَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1151" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Masud (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that a man came to Abdullah ibn Masud and said, &quot;I have divorced my wife by saying I divorce you eight times.&quot; Ibn Masud said to him, &quot;What have people told you?&quot; He replied, &quot;I have been told that I have to part absolutely from her.&quot; Ibn Masud said, &quot;They have spoken the truth. A person who divorces as Allah has commanded, Allah makes it clear for him, and a person who obscures himself in error, we make stay by his error. So do not confuse yourselves and pull us into your confusion. It is as they have said</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1151]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1151" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1151 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">एक शख्स ने इब्न अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) से कहा की मैंने अपनी औरत को सौ तलाकें दें. इब्न अब्बास ने जवाब दिया की वह तीन तलाक़ में तुझसे बा&#x27;इन हो गयी और सत्यान्वे (९७) तलाक़ से तूने अल्लाह की आयतों का मज़ाक उड़ाया. एक शख्स अब्दुल्लाह बिन मसूद के पास आया और कहा मैंने अपनी औरत को दो सौ तलाकें दें. इब्न मसूद ने कहा लोगों ने तुझसे क्या कहा? वह बोला मुझसे ये कहा कि तेरी औरत तुझसे बा&#x27;इन हो गयी. इब्न मसूद ने कहा सच है हुकुम के मुताबिक़ तलाक़ देगा तो अल्लाह ने उसकी सूरत बयां कर दी है और जो गर्बर करेगा उसकी बाला उसके सर लगा देंगे. गर्बर मत करो ताकि हमको मुसीबत न उठाना पड़े</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1151" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ایک شخص نے ابن عباس رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہما سے کہا کہ میں نے اپنی عورت کو سو طلاق دیں ابن عباس نے جواب دیا کہ وہ تین طلاق میں تجھ سے بائن ہوگئی اور ستانوے طلاق سے تو نے اللہ کی آیتوں سے ٹھٹھا کیا ۔، ایک شخص عبداللہ بن مسعود کے پاس آیا اور کہا میں نے اپنی عورت کو دو سو طلاقیں دیں ابن مسعود نے کہا لوگوں نے تجھ سے کیا کہا وہ بولا مجھ سے یہ کہا کہ تیری عورت تجھ سے بائن ہوگئی ابن مسعود نے کہا سچ ہے جو شخص اللہ کے حکم کے موافق طلاق دے گا تو اللہ نے اس کی صورت بیان کر دی اور جو گڑبڑ کرے گا اس کی بلا اس کے سر لگا دیں گے گڑبڑ مت کرو تاکہ ہم کو مصیبت نہ اٹھانا پڑے وہ لوگ سچ کہتے ہیں تیری عورت تجھ سے جدا ہوگئی ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1151" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ek shakhs ne Ibn Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhuma) se kaha ki maine apni aurat ko sau talaqein dein. Ibn Abbas ne jawab diya ki woh teen talaq mein tujhse ba&#x27;in ho gayi aur satyanve (97) talaq se tune Allah ki aayaton ka mazak udaya. Ek shakhs Abdullah bin Masood ke paas aaya aur kaha maine apni aurat ko do sau talaqein dein. Ibn Masood ne kaha logon ne tujhse kya kaha? Woh bola mujhse ye kaha ki teri aurat tujhse ba&#x27;in ho gayi. Ibn Masood ne kaha sach hai, jo shakhs Allah ke hukum ke mutabiq talaq dega to Allah ne uski soorat bayan kar di hai aur jo garbar karega uski bala uske sar laga denge. Garbar mat karo taaki humko museebat na uthana pade, woh log sach kehte hain teri aurat tujhse juda ho gayi.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1151" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1142

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1152)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1152]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1152" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1152</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1152 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ حَزْمٍ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ، قَالَ لَهُ الْبَتَّةُ مَا يَقُولُ النَّاسُ فِيهَا قَالَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ فَقُلْتُ لَهُ كَانَ أَبَانُ بْنُ عُثْمَانَ يَجْعَلُهَا وَاحِدَةً ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ لَوْ كَانَ الطَّلاَقُ أَلْفًا مَا أَبْقَتِ الْبَتَّةُ مِنْهَا شَيْئًا مَنْ قَالَ الْبَتَّةَ فَقَدْ رَمَى الْغَايَةَ الْقُصْوَى ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1152" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz (successor); Aban ibn Uthman (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Abu Bakr ibn Hazm thatUmar ibn Abd al-Aziz had asked him what people said about the &#x27;irrevocable&#x27; divorce, and Abu Bakr had replied that Aban ibn Uthman had clarified that it was declared only once. Umar ibn Abd al- Aziz said, &quot;Even if divorce had to be declared a thousand times, the&#x27;irrevocable&#x27; would use them all up. A person who says, &#x27;irrevocably&#x27; has cast the furthest limit</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1152]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1152" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबू बक्र बिन हज़्म से रिवायत है की उम्र बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने कहा कि तलाक़-इ-बाटता में लोग क्या कहते हैं? अबू बकर ने कहा एबं बिन उस्मान इसे एक तलाक़ समझते थे. उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने कहा अगर तलाक़ एक हज़ार तक दुरुस्त होती तोह बाटता इस में से कुछ बाकी न रखता</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1152" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابو بکر بن حزم سے روایت ہے کہ عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے کہا کہ طلاق بتہ میں لوگ کیا کہتے ہیں ابوبکر نے کہا ابان بن عثمان اس کو ایک طلاق سمجھتے تھے عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے کہا اگر طلاق ایک ہزار تک درست ہوتی تو بتہ اس میں سے کچھ باقی نہ رکھتا جس نے بتہ کہا وہ انتہا کو پہنچ گیا۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Bakr bin Hazm se riwayat hai ki Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne kaha ki talaq-e-batta mein log kya kehte hain? Abu Bakr ne kaha Aban bin Usman ise ek talaq samajhte the. Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne kaha agar talaq ek hazaar tak durust hoti toh batta is mein se kuch baaki na rakhta, jisne batta kaha woh inteha ko pahunch gaya.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّ مَرْوَانَ بْنَ الْحَكَمِ، كَانَ يَقْضِي فِي الَّذِي يُطَلِّقُ امْرَأَتَهُ الْبَتَّةَ أَنَّهَا ثَلاَثُ تَطْلِيقَاتٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَهَذَا أَحَبُّ مَا سَمِعْتُ إِلَىَّ فِي ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Marwan ibn al-Hakam (unresolved). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that Marwan ibn al-Hakam decided that if someone made three pronouncements of divorce, he had divorced his wife irrevocably.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1153]</p>

## Narration 1144

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1154)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1154]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1154</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1154 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّهُ كُتِبَ إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ مِنَ الْعِرَاقَ أَنَّ رَجُلاً قَالَ لاِمْرَأَتِهِ حَبْلُكِ عَلَى غَارِبِكِ فَكَتَبَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ إِلَى عَامِلِهِ أَنْ مُرْهُ يُوَافِينِي بِمَكَّةَ فِي الْمَوْسِمِ فَبَيْنَمَا عُمَرُ يَطُوفُ بِالْبَيْتِ إِذْ لَقِيَهُ الرَّجُلُ فَسَلَّمَ عَلَيْهِ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ مَنْ أَنْتَ فَقَالَ أَنَا الَّذِي أَمَرْتَ أَنْ أُجْلَبَ عَلَيْكَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ عُمَرُ أَسْأَلُكَ بِرَبِّ هَذِهِ الْبَنِيَّةِ مَا أَرَدْتَ بِقَوْلِكَ حَبْلُكِ عَلَى غَارِبِكِ فَقَالَ لَهُ الرَّجُلُ لَوِ اسْتَحْلَفْتَنِي فِي غَيْرِ هَذَا الْمَكَانِ مَا صَدَقْتُكَ أَرَدْتُ بِذَلِكَ الْفِرَاقَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ هُوَ مَا أَرَدْتَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). One report of Umar’s inquiry is preserved in two source passages around the explicit explanatory gloss; no text is rewritten. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Umar ibn al-Khattab had heard in a letter from Iraq that a man said to his wife, &quot;Your rein is on your withers</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr">.&quot; Umar ibn al-Khattab wrote to his governor to order the man to come to him at Makka at the time of hajj. While Umar was doing tawaf around the House, a man met him and greeted him. Umar asked him who he was, and he replied that he was the man that he had ordered to be brought to him. Umar said to him, &quot;I ask you by the Lord of this building, what did you mean by your statement, &#x27;Your rein is on your withers.&#x27;?&quot; The man replied, &quot;Had you made me swear by other than this place, I would not have told you the truth. I intended separation by that.&quot; Umar ibn al- Khattab said, &quot;It is what you intended</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1154]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1154]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1154</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1154 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास खत लिखा हुआ आया कि एक शख्स ने अपनी औरत से कहा &#x27;जबलुकी अला ग़रीबिकी&#x27;। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने लिखा उस शख्स से कह देना कि हज्ज के मौसम में मक्का में मुझसे मिले। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) काबा का तवाफ़ कर रहे थे, एक शख्स मिला और सलाम किया, पूछा तुम कौन हो? आपने फ़रमाया मैं वही शख्स हूँ जिसका तुमने हुकुम किया था मक्का में मिलने का। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा क़सम है तुझको इस घर के रब्ब की, &#x27;जबलुकी अला ग़रीबिकी&#x27; से तेरी क्या मुराद थी? वो बोला ऐ अमीर-उल-मोमिनीन, अगर तुम मुझको किसी और जगह की क़सम देते तो मैं सच न कहता, अब सच कहता हूँ कि मेरी नियत छोड़ देने की थी। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने फ़रमाया जैसे तूने नियत की वैसा ही हुआ।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عمر بن خطاب کے پاس خط لکھا ہوا آیا کہ ایک شخص نے اپنی عورت سے کہا جبلک علی غاربک حضرت عمر خطاب نے لکھا اس شخص سے کہہ دینا کہ حج کے موسم میں مکہ میں مجھ سے ملے حضرت عمر کعبہ کا طواف کر رہے تھے ایک شخص ملا اور سلام کیا پوچھا تم کون ہے آپ نے فرمایا میں وہی شخص ہوں جس نے تم نے حکم کیا تھا مکہ میں ملنے کا حضرت عمر نے کہا قسم ہے تجھ کو اس گھر کے رب کی جبلک علی غاربک سے تیری کیا مراد تھی وہ بولا اے امیر المومین اگر تم مجھ کو کسی اور جگہ کی قسم دیتے تو میں سچ نہ کہتا اب سچ کہتا ہوں کہ میری نیت چھوڑ دینے کی تھی حضرت عمر نے فرمایا جیسے تو نے نیت کی ویسا ہی ہوا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1154]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1154</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1154 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas khat likha hua aaya ki ek shakhs ne apni aurat se kaha &#x27;Jabluki ala gharibiki&#x27;. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne likha us shakhs se keh dena ki hajj ke mausam mein Makkah mein mujhse mile. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) Kaaba ka tawaf kar rahe the, ek shakhs mila aur salam kiya, poochha tum kaun ho? Aapne farmaya main wahi shakhs hoon jiska tumne hukum kiya tha Makkah mein milne ka. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha qasam hai tujhko is ghar ke Rabb ki, &#x27;Jabluki ala gharibiki&#x27; se teri kya murad thi? Woh bola ae Ameer-ul-Momineen, agar tum mujhko kisi aur jagah ki qasam dete toh main sach na kehta, ab sach kehta hoon ki meri niyat chhod dene ki thi. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne farmaya jaise tune niyat ki waisa hi hua.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Ali ibn Abi Talib (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Ali ibn Abi Talib used to say that if a man said to his wife, &quot;You are haram for me,&quot; it counted as three pronouncements of divorce.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1155]</p>

## Narration 1146

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1156)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1156]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1156" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1156</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1156 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ يَقُولُ فِي الْخَلِيَّةِ وَالْبَرِيَّةِ إِنَّهَا ثَلاَثُ تَطْلِيقَاتٍ كُلُّ وَاحِدَةٍ مِنْهُمَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1156" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>
